Safety definitions and counts

Safety describes the known effect, not whether zipmi permits execution. When one recovered firmware row combines behaviors, the displayed class is the highest known impact and the row says so.

Read-Only 40 operations

Reads state and has no known persistent or service effect.

Sensitive 1 operations

Reads or changes credentials, trust, sessions, raw memory, firmware-control state, or other security-sensitive material.

State-Changing 91 operations

Changes configuration or runtime state without a known service interruption or data deletion.

Disruptive 4 operations

Can interrupt a service, session, device, or management connection.

Destructive 0 operations

Can erase data, reset configuration, or replace firmware or persistent state.

Unknown 317 operations

Available evidence does not establish the external effect.

Inventory scope and known gaps

External or indirect handler boundaries are labeled explicitly. Runtime data can replace the embedded BIOS translation table, and framework registration order does not statically resolve the 0x30/0xe2 provider collision.

Live evidence

Only read-only requests were attempted. NetFn 0x30/Cmd 0xe2 timed out and left the emulated IPMI service unhealthy, so the route is classified disruptive and gated. No credential-bearing or mutating command was sent.

Commands to send, payload layouts, safety, availability, zipmi support, and evidence.

4CommerMEOEMGetReading / PnmOemGetReading
30/e2

Colliding IEIT common-interface and Intel PNM reading providers.

Disruptive

Can suppress the reply and leave the emulated IPMI service unhealthy.

zipmi oem ieit --unsafe 'CommerMEOEMGetReading / PnmOemGetReading'

Status: Complete

Length: Variable length; see the operation contract

Summary: Provider-dependent sequence of 3-byte reading descriptors.

Request fields

No fields.

Status: Complete

Length: See response contract

Summary: Provider-dependent 4-byte reading records; an empty live request produced no reply.

Response fields

No fields.

Privilege: User

Interface: Raw OEM NetFn over authenticated IPMI; no IANA prefix

Available on this firmware: Statically registered in the pinned firmware.

Completion codes: Not separately recovered

Named operation route: Requires --unsafe

Captured live request: transport timeout after 20 seconds; subsequent IPMI requests also timed out; The nominal getter can block the single emulated IPMI service when its ME backend is absent. It must require --unsafe despite read-like names and privileges.

Recovered from

libipmipdkcmds.so.6.1.0:g_Oem_NetFn30_CmdHndlr[0] → CommerMEOEMGetReading; libipminmsupport.so.6.6.0:g_PNM_CmdHndlr[0] → PnmOemGetReading

Confidence: high collision; runtime winner unresolved
